#!/bin/bash

Help(){
    :
}

Error(){
    echo "[ FAIL ] Invalid command"
}

while true
do
    echo -n "ftp> "
    read -r CMD

    case $CMD in

        'help') Help ;;
        'quit') break ;;
        *     ) Error ;;
    esac
done[root@main ~]# c

ashrc
# .bashrc# Source global definitionsif [ -f /etc/bashrc ]; then. /etc/bashrc
fi

# User specific environment
if ! [[ "$PATH" =~ "$HOME/.local/bin:$HOME/bin:" ]]
then
    PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$HOME/bin:$PATH"
fi
PATH=$PATH:/root/shell
export PATH

# Uncomment the following line if you don't like systemctl's auto-paging feature:
# export SYSTEMD_PAGER=

# User specific aliases and functions

alias rm='rm -i'
alias cp='cp -i'
alias mv='mv -i'
alias c='clear'

#
# Sfecific configuration
#
export PS1='\[\e[34;1m\][\u@\h\[\e[33;1m\] \w\[\e[34;1m\]]\$ \[\e[m\]'

alias code='code --user-data-dir $HOME/vscode --no-sandbox --password-store="basic"'
alias vscode='code'


#
# Temporary Alias
#

[root@main ~]# cat /root/bin/calculator_ver1.sh
#!/bin/bash

plus(){
    echo "$A + $B = $(expr $A + $B)"
}

Minus(){
    echo "$A - $B = $(expr $A - $B)"
}

Mul(){
    echo "$A x $B = $(expr $A \* $B)"
}

Div(){
    echo "$A / $B = $(expr $A / $B)"
}

read -p "Enter A  : " A
read -p "Enter Op : " OP
read -p "Enter B  : " B

case "$OP" in
    '+') plus  ;;
    '-') Minus ;;
    '*') Mul   ;;
    '/') Div   ;;
     * ) echo "[ FAIL ]"
         exit 1 ;;
esac

echo "임의로 만든 것 $A $OP $B = $(expr $A "$OP" $B)"[root@main ~]#

#!/bin/bash

/bin/cp -p /etc/hosts /root/bin/hosts

#HOSTS=/etc/hosts 아래 걸로 정상 실행 될 경우 주석해제하고 아래 주석 해제
HOSTS=/root/bin/hosts

grep 'Linux Servers' $HOSTS > /dev/null 2>&1 || cat <<'EOF' >> $HOSTS

#
# Linux Servers
#

EOF
